Title: Catechesis Ecclesiarum quae in Regno Poloniae et magno Ducatu Lithuaniae, et aliis ad istud Regnum pertinentibus Provinciis, affirmant, neminem alium, praeter Patrem Domini nostri Iesu Christi, esse illum unum Deum Israelis (the Racovian Catechism, first Latin edition)
Creator: Racovian Catechism (the Catechism of the Polish Brethren; Latin version by Hieronim Moskorzewski, named as translator in the host record)
Publisher: Racow: Sebastian Sternacki, 1609
Publication Date: 1609
Rights: Public Domain (published 1609; public domain by date; host rights field "Domena publiczna (public domain)")
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item jbc.bj.uj.edu.pl.NDIGSTDR003939, file NDIGSTDR003939_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, one-world agent for anti-trinitarian-currents-servetus-polish-brethr), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: "Domena publiczna (public domain)" (contributor Biblioteka Jagiellonska, collections polishpdbooks and polishpublicdomain).
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from long-s to s normalisation (0 occurrences). Rights basis restated: a 1609 Racow imprint, read off the scanned title page, is public domain by date, and the host marks it public domain.
Content note: The whole 1609 Latin Catechism with its dedicatory Praefatio to King James I of Great Britain, then the questions and answers in chapters (including De Via Salutis, De Persona Christi, De Officio Christi, De Ecclesia). This is the earlier recension; Rees's English (1818) translates the 1680 recension, so the two are not the same text. OCR sampled at five points: Latin readable, but with long-s read as f (the word-like share of tokens is about 0.86), scattered Cyrillic-looking glyphs for short Latin words and some doubled columns; quote only after checking the page image. Second witness for exact wording until a cleaner scan is found (Mark's OCR ruling, 2026-09-25).
